- [ ] **Step 3: Record the decisions and the deferrals**

Append to `docs/plans/phase-0-followups.md`:

````markdown

## Phase 1a (homogeneous-sphere Mie): decisions and deferrals

Resolved from the lists above: the complex reciprocal (Smith; complex division, `csqrt` and
`ln` near 1 wait for a consumer), Riccati–Bessel and log-derivative functions (inside
`scatrs::mie`), the `allow_unvalidated` mechanism with the first accuracy map, the Mie Arb
generator with a proven tail bound, the data-budget test, rustdoc in CI, the four doctests,
the `dd/traits.rs` link, the docs-consistency extensions and `packaging` in the `test` extra.

Decisions (spec `docs/specs/2026-10-13-phase-1-mie-design.md`, section 12):
- Truncation ⌈x + 6x^(1/3) + 3⌉ (f64) and ⌈x + 12x^(1/3) + 6⌉ (DoubleDouble), measured.
- Dₙ(mx) by a contracting downward recurrence: no Lentz continued fraction, no `NotConverged`.
- Q_ext = Q_sca + Q_abs, with Q_abs from the per-term identity.
- Tolerance C·(κ′ + cond)·2⁻⁵², enforced by `mie_reference.rs` and `mie_accuracy_map.rs`.
- The reference generator avoids Arb's Bessel functions and uses disk arithmetic.

Phase 1b must include (besides spec section 9):
- **Performance.** The Jacobian costs 1.8× the values at large x: profile the per-order
  derivative work (three complex reciprocals per order in the ratio region). Measure Mie on
  x86_64 with and without FMA (the f64 kernel uses no `mul_add`, but `DoubleDouble` maps do).
- **Derivative series tail bound.** Certify the series of derivatives, for example with
  Cauchy's estimate in m (a disk of m in the tail bound of Proposition 3).
- **Larger certified cases.** Series records with |m|x > 6·10⁴ are high-precision (9 of 306);
  a faster generator (fewer Python-level operations per order) would certify them.
- **Accuracy-map cost.** The map takes about 200 CPU-seconds in the test profile; if CI time
  matters, sample derivative conditions on every second point.
- **∂Q_abs/∂x and ∂Q_abs/∂(Re m)** of weakly absorbing spheres are accurate only relative to
  Q_ext (relative error up to 4·10⁻⁹ at k = 10⁻⁸ near a resonance): investigate a formulation
  accurate relative to Q_abs.
- **First real CI run.** The Mie accuracy map and reference tests run on Windows for the first
  time there; libm sin/cos differences change last digits only, but check the printed worst
  constants.
````

## Phase 1a exit checklist (spec §1.1 and main spec §13)

- [x] Accuracy map for coefficients, efficiencies and amplitudes (and their derivatives) published: `docs/validation/accuracy.md` (Task 5).
- [x] Declared validated domain enforced: `scatrs/tests/mie_reference.rs` and `scatrs/tests/mie_accuracy_map.rs` (Task 5), both inside `cargo test --workspace`, which CI runs on Linux, macOS and Windows. Green locally on macOS; the first CI run is pending (the repository has no remote yet).
- [x] Analytic and `Dual` derivatives agree: `closed_form_derivatives_agree_with_dual` (Task 4).
- [x] Mie Arb references certified with a proven tail bound where n_ref ≤ 60 000, labels never overstated (Tasks 2–3).
- [x] Python `scatrs.mie.coefficients`, `efficiencies`, `amplitudes`, `default_n_max` (Task 6).
- [ ] Jacobian overhead ≤ 2× the values (Task 7): met for absorbing spheres (1.2–1.8×), missed
  for non-absorbing spheres with m ≲ 1.4 (2.0× at m = 1.5, up to 2.2× at m = 0.75, for
  x ≥ 1000). Recorded for phase 1b; accepting it for 1a is the user's decision.
- [x] Phase 0 carry-overs done (Task 1).

## Execution notes (2026-10-13)

Each task was executed as written above, then reviewed for spec compliance and code quality.
Review fixes followed as separate commits and changed the plan's code as follows:

- **Task 1** (0355f27, ca673c9): doctest placement and comments.
- **Task 2** (19f8acd, ea34f9b): a sharper disk-arithmetic test (the planned one also passed
  against a broken disk) and documentation.
- **Task 3** (8c682de, fbf3aac): tail-bound tests that fail when the bound is wrong (one passed
  vacuously), a test of the threshold regime, `DiskContainsZero`, and a docs link the strict
  build rejected.
- **Task 4** (3accb3d, d043463): non-finite `Dual` derivative parts rejected with
  `InvalidInput`; the documentation of g at m = 1; property-test coverage.
- **Task 5** (b2b327b, d242b79, f30f6cf): the accuracy model changed (spec section 12): cond
  componentwise for the real outputs, κ′ per output, constants 16, 64, 256 and 512;
  `DoubleDouble` checked against Arb on every output; guards against NaN, empty record sets and
  degenerate measures; corrections to the theory and accuracy pages.
- **Task 6** (a96fdb4, 650fea4, ee6b8df): fresh views for every input; every output bounded
  before allocation, the efficiencies included; `scatrs::mie::n_terms`; per-element errors from
  all three functions; read-only results; `default_n_max` validates x; more tests; the example
  page's absorption check.
- **Task 7** (871787a, f13ef83): benchmark cases at real m, where the Jacobian reaches 2.0×
  the values; figures refreshed from the commit's own runs; criterion 0.7 (rust-version 1.80,
  no C compiler) instead of 0.8; a CI step that runs each benchmark once.
- **Task 8** (9a60c36, c8df42f, 7bff087): as planned, then the review items deferred to it (the
  docs-consistency fixes, two small fixes, the follow-up notes in `phase-0-followups.md`) and
  this record, with the spec's section 12.
- **Final review** (the commit after 7bff087): the validated domain requires |m| ≥ 0.1, with
  κ built on max(1, |m|)·x and C = 128 for S₁, S₂ (it was false for |m| ≲ 0.2, where nothing
  sampled); four indices below |m| = 1 in the map; no 32-bit overflow in k(k + 1); anchored
  docs-consistency contracts tied to the domain and limits in code; `allow_unvalidated` must
  be a bool; a test of the accepted corners; wording (CI not yet run, the Jacobian cost at
  real m, the reference labels).
